# Block Geometry and Visual Bindings

Model names below are appearance identifiers, not a required rendering format. Coordinates are in sixteenths of a block. Each element is an axis-aligned box before its listed rotation; each face binds to the stated texture and texture coordinates. A parent inherits geometry, bindings and display transforms; child bindings override parent bindings. A # texture is a binding reference. External parent geometry and textures are explicit baseline dependencies, not target-engine defaults. Original texture images under the shared assets tree are the pixel-accurate visual reference; Markdown geometry alone cannot reproduce their pixels. No new image assets are generated by this specification.

## block/acacia_cabinet

| Visual field | Exact value |
| --- | --- |
| parent | `minecraft:block/orientable` |
| textures / front | `farmersdelight:block/acacia_cabinet_front` |
| textures / side | `farmersdelight:block/acacia_cabinet_side` |
| textures / top | `farmersdelight:block/acacia_cabinet_top` |
